# LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 -- the values-check of the amendment (the head of LOCK-m-F0.md, 377 lines at the commit; each pass read the
# draft as it then stood, so the returns' line numbers are that draft's), its PIN-index addendum and the RESULTS entry, before
# the commit: two adversarial passes, each with verifiers (one per range), strict refuters on every flagged claim, and a
# values lens, run as a Code workflow. The journals' returns follow verbatim (JSON), with Code's dispositions per pass. Every
# confirmed correction and every values finding was applied, or given a disposition below, before the commit.

The workflow script (both passes ran it; PASS from args): /Users/jasondwyer/.claude/projects/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/workflows/scripts/lock-v02-valuescheck-wf_73058fb5-ae7.js
export const meta = {
  name: 'lock-v02-valuescheck',
  description: 'Values-check the LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 amendment, its PIN-index addendum and the LOCK v0.2 RESULTS entry: claim verifiers, strict refuters, a values and completeness lens',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'three verifiers extract and check every atomic claim' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'a strict refuter per flagged claim batch' },
    { title: 'Values', detail: 'a values and completeness lens' },
  ],
}
const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const PY = REPO + '/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python'
const PASS = args && args.pass ? args.pass : 1
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO} (PROPOSAL-m, a pre-registered experiment; Code = the build/run role, the design seat adjudicates, the PI routes). Step 13: Code has drafted LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 (uncommitted; values-check pass ${PASS} before commit). Three texts to check:
 (L) the working-tree LOCK-m-F0.md: the v0.2 AMENDMENT is everything from its first line down to (and including) the paragraph under "# The v0.1 record"; after that paragraph the file must be the v0.1 text byte-for-byte (git show e3bef6f:LOCK-m-F0.md; sha256 1a043c287da1f01a0c72b347f3170e499eeef17a63aea603adc02d110dc38b6b). Check the amendment, not the v0.1 text (except to confirm it is untouched and that "lock §N" references resolve to it).
 (A) results/raw/2026-10-13_m-lock-v0.2_pin-index-addendum.txt (untracked), the PIN-index addendum v0.2 declares; check its header and that each listed block equals the marked lines of scripts/m_sessions.py at commit 8da32f2 (blob eb6aa7cf167aa8d955c26fd97a6214ed68a7f1375b51c9f80582c85ed659fd6c, = HEAD's) at the stated line, and each step-10 line at 0fe65b9; the ten v0.1 sites against results/raw/2026-10-10_m-lock-v0.1_pin-index.txt (P256-P265).
 (E) the new top entry of RESULTS.md, "2026-10-13 — LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 ..." (from the line after "## Log — Phase 2" down to the line before "- 2026-10-13 — **G-ID-F0m").
The rulings v0.2 must carry out: the seat's step-10 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md (its Q1-Q7, §2's six items and the values paragraph, "What Code does now" 3) and the seat's G-ID-F0m adjudication results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-ID-F0m_adjudication.md (Q1-Q4, §5, §6). The PI's routing of 2026-10-13 is quoted in (L) and (E) (treat it as given; curly apostrophes ’; it ends "onto step 14" with no full stop). Other sources: PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N = line N); RESULTS.md's G-ID-F0m entry and its close line, the G-TIME-F0 entry and its close line, the LOCK v0.1 entry; valence_core/m/*.py, scripts/m_sessions.py, valence_core/adapters/qwen_mlx.py at HEAD (64f3c7e); data/m/gates/Q-F0_G-ID.json, data/m/reports/*.json (sham-only reads readable under m:318 exception (4)); results/raw/2026-10-08_note-N17-numerics.md; the clone jlens-qwen36/jlens_qwen/*.py (read-only).
HARD RULES: read-only. Do NOT edit any file. No git command that writes. Never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36. Do NOT load a model; do not run the driver's stages or self-test. NEVER open, print, parse or read data/m/ckpt/* (hashing with shasum is allowed). For python use plain python3 over files, or ${PY} -I -c for read-only checks that need valence_core.`
const CLAIMS = { type: 'object', properties: { claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' },
  verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' },
}, required: ['id', 'text', 'where', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['claims'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' },
}, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['verdicts'] }
const VALUES = { type: 'object', properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] }, where: { type: 'string' }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' },
}, required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } }, required: ['findings'] }
const PARTS = [
  { key: 'L1', scope: 'the amendment of (L), its title through the end of "## v0.2 §2" (header and status, the form, how a stage cites the file, the conventions, the path and the kernel statement, the projections and every number in them, including Code\'s computed hours and the 3.25 h)' },
  { key: 'L2', scope: 'the amendment of (L) from "## v0.2 §3" through the "# The v0.1 record" paragraph (the driver and its history, PIN-S10 as read, Q2\'s conditions, the addendum\'s description, the weights of record, the operand-exclusion sentence -- check it is byte-for-byte lock §3.9\'s draft --, the six items\' table and every quote in it, the process rulings, what follows, every row of the record tables), AND the addendum (A)' },
  { key: 'E', scope: 'the entry (E), every claim, against (L), (A), the rulings and the repository; the entry states the lock\'s sha256 and line count and the addendum\'s sha256 -- recompute them on the working tree' },
]
const results = await pipeline(
  PARTS,
  p => agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (verifier ${p.key}, pass ${PASS}). Extract EVERY atomic factual claim in ${p.scope} -- every number, hash, path, commit, line anchor, quote, attribution, count, date, and every statement about what code does or what a ruling says -- and check each against the sources with tools. Quotes must be verbatim (say exactly where they differ). Attributions must be exact: a reading presented as the seat's must be the seat's words; Code's readings must be marked as Code's. Verdict per claim: VERIFIED, FAILS, IMPRECISE, UNCHECKABLE, with evidence and, for FAILS/IMPRECISE, an exact correction. Be exhaustive.`,
    { label: `verify:${p.key}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS }),
  (res, p) => {
    const flagged = (res && res.claims ? res.claims : []).filter(c => c.verdict === 'FAILS' || c.verdict === 'IMPRECISE')
    if (flagged.length === 0) return { part: p.key, claims: res ? res.claims : [], refuted: [] }
    return agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (STRICT refuter, ${p.key}, pass ${PASS}). A verifier flagged the claims below as FAILS or IMPRECISE. For EACH, independently re-check against the sources and try to REFUTE the flag. CONFIRMED = the flag stands (give the exact corrected wording, minimal); REFUTED = the claim was right (say why); PLAUSIBLE = unsettled.
FLAGGED:
${JSON.stringify(flagged, null, 1)}`, { label: `refute:${p.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: REF })
      .then(v => ({ part: p.key, claims: res.claims, refuted: v ? v.verdicts : [] }))
  }
)
const values = await agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (values and completeness lens, pass ${PASS}). Read (L)'s amendment and (E) whole, as the design seat and the PI will. (1) COMPLETENESS: list every item the step-10 read's "What Code does now" 3, its Q1-Q7 (where they bear on v0.2), its §2 (the six items, the values paragraph) and the adjudication's §5, Q1-Q4 and §6 require v0.2 to carry; for each, say where v0.2 carries it, or that it is missing or carried differently than ruled. (2) FIDELITY: misattribution (Code's vs the seat's vs the PI's vs the proposal's); anything stated as ruled that the rulings leave to Code or leave open (e.g. the form of the step-14 refusal is Code's at the edit); any new Code reading not marked as Code's; selective quotation that shifts a ruling's scope. (3) CORRECTNESS OF FORM: the v0.1 text untouched; nothing truncated; check (f)'s separation (the amendment must not name the analyzer's or the package's hash in full); a stage citing v0.2 must still pass the licence. (4) Anything unclear to a reader without Code's session. Give each finding with suggested replacement text.`,
  { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { pass: PASS, parts: results, values: values ? values.findings : [] }
